<h3><a href="https://www.threads.com/@saars.foto/post/DfACewYHAF5">Pattern Tuesday 🧩 Show me a pattern. Repeats, rhythms, grids, stripes - patterns hide in plain sight. Show me the one your eye keeps coming back to. This facade is just one long rhythm of fins - find me a pattern. ONE photo. Replying is consent: 3 favorites featured tomorrow in a Threads post and an Instagram reel, pinned to the profile, with full credit. Standout replies may also appear in an occasional best-of-the-week reel. What pattern did you walk past today?</a></h3>
